# List of American films of 2026

This is a list of [American films](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/American_film "American film") that are scheduled for release in [2026](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_in_film "2026 in film").

Following the box office section, this list is organized chronologically, providing information on release dates, production companies, directors, and principal cast members.

## Box office

The highest-grossing American films released in 2026, by domestic box office [gross revenue](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gross_revenue "Gross revenue"), are as follows:

† *Background shading and* † *indicates films currently playing.*

| Rank | Title | Distributor | Domestic gross |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| 2 | *[The Odyssey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Odyssey_(2026_film) "The Odyssey (2026 film)")* † | [Universal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Universal_Pictures "Universal Pictures") | $624,348,825 |
| 3 | *[Toy Story 5](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Toy_Story_5 "Toy Story 5")* | [Disney](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Walt_Disney_Studios_Motion_Pictures "Walt Disney Studios Motion Pictures") | $480,294,461 |

| 6 | *[Project Hail Mary](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Project_Hail_Mary_(film) "Project Hail Mary (film)")* | [Amazon MGM](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amazon_MGM_Studios "Amazon MGM Studios") | $344,050,007 |
| 7 | *[Obsession](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Obsession_(2025_film) "Obsession (2025 film)")* | [Focus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Focus_Features "Focus Features") | $263,451,715 |
| 8 | *[The Devil Wears Prada 2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Devil_Wears_Prada_2 "The Devil Wears Prada 2")* | [20th Century](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/20th_Century_Studios "20th Century Studios") | $220,556,651 |
